3	**Gathered:** 2026-07-01
4	**Status:** Ready for planning
5	
6	<domain>
7	## Phase Boundary
8	
9	Cut the **web app** over to the v2.0 read key-chaining rotation model. Three deliverables (ROT-07 + ROADMAP SC 1–5):
10	
11	1. All revocation-triggering mutations (delete, move, rename-on-scope-exit) call `rotateReadFromNode`; `executeLazyRotation` is deleted and `addShareKeys` / `reWrapForRecipients` are removed from per-mutation fan-out.
12	2. A **durable IndexedDB** anti-rollback floor — `{nodeId → highestGeneration}` (M1) and `{nodeId → highestSeq}` (§6.5) — that survives page reload and fails closed on regression.
13	3. `folderTree` is reconciled against the current `sequenceNumber` **before** any rotation publish; reconcile failure defers (never publishes rotation on stale state).
14	
15	This is the **web-side integration** of engine/crypto already built in Phases 62–67. No new crypto primitives, no schema changes. UI hint: **yes** (rotation progress + offline/fail-closed messaging → follow-up `/gsd-ui-phase 68`).
16	
17	**Not this phase:** FUSE/WinFsp/Rust integration and the Q3 FUSE-side authority mirror → **Phase 69**. The rotation engine internals (already sound as of Phase 64). Server-side generation gate (Phase 66, shipped).
18	
19	</domain>
20	
21	<decisions>
22	## Implementation Decisions
23	
24	### Rotation UX — co-writer offline (Q1) & progress
25	- **D-01 (Q1 — offline co-writer):** Explicit **fail-closed error + one-tap "Refresh access"**. A stale/rotated-out co-writer's write fails closed with a clear message and a "Refresh access" action that re-resolves the write descriptor. If they were rotated out, the message escalates to "write access revoked." No silent grace window — honors the explicit WRITE-03 / ADR 0001 model.
26	- **D-02 (progress):** **Synchronous fast root cut + background badge for the tail walk.** The root cut (the actual revocation for the revoked reader) completes synchronously with a spinner; the `O(items)` tail runs as a background, resumable walk with a persistent "Finishing revocation…" indicator that survives reload. Matches the Phase 63 Q2 decision (web = first-class best-effort host; long multi-session web rotation for large revokes is an accepted documented limitation).
27	- **D-03 (badge placement):** **Global app-header/status badge**, visible across folder navigation (the walk spans a subtree, not one folder), with a "Resuming revocation…" state after reload. Exact copy and visual spec **deferred to `/gsd-ui-phase 68`**.
28	
29	### Reconcile & fail-closed behavior
30	- **D-04 (reconcile failure — SC#3):** **Defer, never skip.** If `folderTree` reconcile against the current `sequenceNumber` fails, the mutation **defers** (does not publish a rotation on stale state) with an auto-retrying "Syncing latest state…" notice. The `#489`/`#494` desync class must not produce a silent missed revocation.
31	- **D-05 (regression detected — SC#1/#4):** **Hard fail-closed.** A generation or seq regression from the relay raises a hard fail-closed error toast ("stale data from server rejected") — never silent acceptance. Surfaced as a **per-mutation toast**, not a global page block.
32	- **D-06 (deferred-mutation terminal behavior):** **Bounded backoff → terminal error + manual retry; nothing queued.** Auto-retry the defer path with backoff for a bounded window (target ~5 attempts / ~30s — exact numbers are Claude's discretion); on exhaustion surface a terminal "couldn't complete securely — retry" with a manual retry action. The mutation is **not** applied (fail-closed, SC#3). **No durable retry queue** (that would be scope creep).
33	
34	### Durable client state (ROT-07 / M1 + §6.5)
35	- **D-07 (storage):** Persist both high-water maps (`{nodeId → highestGeneration}`, `{nodeId → highestSeq}`) in a **dedicated IndexedDB object store**, reusing the existing `idb` wrapper pattern (`search-index.service.ts`, `lib/device/identity.ts`), keyed by `nodeId`, **monotonic-max** on write. The generation floor is **seeded from the grant's `rootGeneration`** (owner-vouched) on first contact. Store name/version/eviction = Claude's discretion.
36	- **D-08 (IndexedDB unavailable/cleared):** **Degrade to the design §4.3 first-contact path, warn once.** When the durable store is unavailable (private mode) or was cleared, fall back: seed from grant `rootGeneration` and cross-check the envelope `generation` against the parent `SealedChildRef.generation` mirror + `versionFloor`; hold an in-memory session floor. Reads continue and anti-rollback is still held by the signed parent chain. Show a **one-time** "secure cache unavailable" notice. (The colluding-relay self-consistent-old-snapshot residual is already an accepted irreducible per §4.3.)
37	- **D-09 (multi-tab coordination):** **`navigator.locks` leader + monotonic-max high-water.** One tab is elected to drive the tail walk; other tabs observe. High-water writes are monotonic-max (last-write-wins is safe). If Web Locks is unavailable, **both tabs run idempotently** — still safe via the idempotent walk + CAS-409 re-merge (Phase 64 D-07: double-rotation only strengthens revocation).
38	
39	### Q3 — write-recipient-vs-owner sub-share authority (web mirror)
40	- **D-10 (authority — mirrors Phase 65 D-01):** Crypto is locked upstream: write-recipient **C** unlinks + bins with **no cross-principal revoke attempt and no new schema**. The **owner's** reconcile+rotation pass re-derives dangling grants from the existing `shares WHERE rootNodeId ∈ destroyed/binned-subtree` enumeration (the inverted HIGH-3 `reMintGrantsRootedAt` seam). Phase 68 wires this owner reconcile **live on web** (Phase 65 built the mock-tested seam; Phase 66 cut over the `shares` schema).
41	- **D-11 (owner reconcile cadence):** **Eager — on login/app-open + opportunistically after the owner's own mutations.** Minimizes the dangling-grant window with no new schema. **C receives no advisory**; the exposure window is already documented (ADR 0002 — read-revoke protects future content/navigation only).
42	
43	### Cutover scope (from ROADMAP success criteria)
44	- **D-12 (delete list — SC#2):** Delete `executeLazyRotation` from `apps/web/src/services/share.service.ts`; remove `addShareKeys` and `reWrapForRecipients` from per-mutation fan-out paths. All revocation-triggering mutations route through `rotateReadFromNode`. Fold in the Phase 64 OUT-tagged `sdk-client-move-publish-durability` work (dest-before-source publish ordering + unreadable-descendant enumeration) since it ties into `folderTree`/sequence reconcile.
45	- **D-13 (test extension — SC#5):** All new web test files use `.test.ts` (never `.spec.ts`). apps/web vitest `include` is `*.test.ts` only — `.spec.ts` files are silently skipped in CI. `find apps/web/src -name "*.spec.ts"` must return empty.
46	
47	### Claude's Discretion
48	- Exact retry counts / backoff curve for D-06.
49	- IndexedDB store name, schema version, and eviction policy for D-07.
50	- Badge copy, visual treatment, and per-state text (D-03) → resolved at `/gsd-ui-phase 68`.
51	
52	</decisions>

95	### Established Patterns
96	- **Web/SDK folder-state desync:** state lives in BOTH the Zustand store (`apps/web/src/stores/folder.store.ts`) and the SDK `folderTree`; reconcile `folderTree` before sdk-core mutations — IPNS `sequenceNumber` is the clock (backs D-04).
97	- **Transport-decoupled share callbacks:** grant issuance/persistence goes through injected callbacks (Phase 63 D-05 / Phase 64 D-04) — the owner reconcile (D-10) wires the real `shares` persistence (schema cut over in Phase 66) behind that seam.
98	- **Fail-closed IPNS resolve:** the verified-resolver chokepoint (v1.1 HARD block) is where the seq high-water hooks in (D-05).

102	- `apps/web/src/services/ipns.service.ts` — `resolveIpnsRecord` web resolve path; wire the durable `{nodeId → highestSeq}` high-water check here (SC#4 / D-05).
103	- `apps/web/src/stores/folder.store.ts` — `folderTree` / `sequenceNumber` reconcile-before-publish (D-04).
104	- Mutation call sites (delete/move/rename) in `apps/web/src/hooks/` (`useFolderMutations`, `useFileOperations`, `useFileBrowserActions`) route to `rotateReadFromNode` on scope exit.

16	## Scope Note
17	
18	**This phase is primarily backend/client-state (rotation engine wiring, durable IndexedDB high-water state, `folderTree` reconciliation).** The UI surface is intentionally narrow — five notification/status states, all reusing the app's existing notification and header primitives. No new pages, dialogs, or layouts. This contract covers only:
19	
20	1. A global rotation-progress status badge (app header) — D-02/D-03
21	2. Four toast-notification variants riding the existing `NotificationToast` component — D-01, D-04, D-05, D-06, D-08
22	
23	If a reviewer expects more UI surface than this, they are looking at the wrong phase — see Phase 69 (FUSE/desktop) and Phase 63/65 (crypto engine, no UI).

93	## Copywriting Contract
94	
95	All copy is plain, terse, and matches the existing `[INFO]`/`[WARN]`/`[ERR]` label convention in `NotificationToast.tsx`. No new tone — no exclamation points, no marketing language, consistent with the rest of the app's terminal aesthetic.
96	
97	### Rotation status badge (D-02/D-03) — mounted in `AppHeader`'s `.header-right`, before `UserMenu`
98	
99	| State | Trigger | Label | Visual |
100	|-------|---------|-------|--------|
101	| Hidden | No rotation active for this session | *(not rendered)* | — |
102	| Root cut in progress | `rotateReadFromNode` root step running (synchronous) | `Revoking access…` | Spinner + green (`--color-green-primary`) border, non-dismissible |
103	| Tail walk in progress | Background subtree walk running after root cut completes | `Finishing revocation…` | Static pill, green border, no spinner (avoid implying it's blocking) |
104	| Resuming after reload | Durable job record found mid-walk on page load | `Resuming revocation…` | Same pill styling as tail-walk state |
105	
106	Badge is a status pill only — not a button, not focusable, no click action (D-03 explicitly deferred any interactivity; it is informational). `role="status"`, `aria-live="polite"` (never `assertive` — it is not an error).
107	
108	### Toast notifications (extend `NotificationToast.tsx` / `notification.store.ts`)
109	
110	| Element | Copy | Type | Notes |
111	|---------|------|------|-------|
112	| Reconcile-defer notice (D-04) | `Syncing latest state…` | `info` | Auto-retrying in background; auto-dismisses on success (no manual dismiss needed, but existing `[x]` stays available) |
113	| Regression rejected (D-05) | `Stale data from server rejected.` | `error` | Per-mutation toast, not a full-page block; the mutation itself did not apply |
114	| Defer exhausted / terminal (D-06) | `Couldn't complete securely — retry.` | `error` | Requires an action button labeled `Retry` (see Component Extension below); mutation was never applied |
115	| Offline/stale co-writer write failure (D-01, non-revoked) | `Write failed — access may be out of date.` | `error` | Requires an action button labeled `Refresh access` |
116	| Offline/stale co-writer, actually revoked (D-01, escalated) | `Write access revoked.` | `error` | No action button — refreshing cannot help; this is terminal |
117	| IndexedDB unavailable (D-08), shown once per session | `Secure cache unavailable — falling back to server verification.` | `warning` | One-time only; do not re-show on every subsequent mutation this session |
118

121	`Notification` type (`apps/web/src/stores/notification.store.ts`) needs an optional action field to support D-01 (`Refresh access`) and D-06 (`Retry`):

125	  id: string;
126	  type: 'info' | 'warning' | 'error';
127	  message: string;
128	  createdAt: number;
129	  action?: { label: string; onClick: () => void };
130	};
131	```
132	
133	`NotificationToast.tsx` renders the action as a small text button matching the existing `[x]` dismiss button's styling (same font, `--color-text-muted` default / `--color-green-primary` on hover, no background, no border-radius) — placed before the `[x]` dismiss button, e.g. `[Retry]` `[x]`. Do not use a filled/primary button style; the whole toast system is text-only chrome.

444	## Security Domain
445	
446	### Applicable ASVS Categories
447	
448	| ASVS Category | Applies | Standard Control |
449	|---------------|---------|-------------------|
450	| V2 Authentication | No | Out of scope — this phase does not touch login/session |
451	| V3 Session Management | No | Out of scope |
452	| V4 Access Control | Yes | This phase's entire purpose is access-control soundness (read-key rotation on scope exit) — the control is `hasCoveringGrant` + `rotateReadFromNode`, already built; this phase must not weaken the "either source covering ⇒ rotate" invariant |
453	| V5 Input Validation | Yes | The new IndexedDB high-water values must be validated on read (reject non-numeric/negative/malformed stored values as if the store were absent — same posture as `identity.ts`'s "validate payload shape before touching crypto" pattern) before use in a fail-closed comparison |
454	| V6 Cryptography | Yes (indirectly) | No new crypto primitives are introduced — this phase only *invokes* existing AEAD/ECIES primitives (`wrapKey`, `sealChildReadKey`) inside already-built engine code; never hand-roll comparison logic that substitutes for AAD binding (see Pitfall 5) |
455	
456	### Known Threat Patterns for this phase
457	
458	| Pattern | STRIDE | Standard Mitigation |
459	|---------|--------|----------------------|
460	| Colluding relay withholds/replays a stale signed IPNS record post-revocation (design §4.3 M1) | Spoofing / Tampering | Durable client-side `{nodeId→highestGeneration}` high-water, fail-closed on regression (ROT-07 — this phase's core deliverable) |
461	| Colluding relay serves a within-generation stale-sequence record (design §6.5) | Tampering | Durable `{nodeId→highestSeq}` high-water, fail-closed on regression |
462	| Relay omits a grant root from `activeGrantRootIpnsNames` to suppress rotation (T-63-17) | Repudiation / Elevation of privilege | `hasCoveringGrant` cross-checks `localGrantRecord` independently — this phase must actually populate `localGrantRecord` from real client-held grant state (tie-in to the share.service.ts fetch-stub gap, Pitfall 2) or the cross-check is vacuously always-relay-trusting |
463	| Stale `folderTree` causes a rotation publish on top of already-superseded state, silently missing a revocation (the `#489`/`#494` desync class) | Tampering / DoS-of-security-guarantee | Reconcile-before-publish (D-04), defer never skip |
464	| A revoked reader observes the rotation-progress badge/toast timing to infer subtree size or in-progress state | Information disclosure (low severity) | UI-SPEC already scopes the badge to `aria-live="polite"`, non-interactive, no per-item detail exposed — no new work needed, just don't regress this in implementation |
465	
466	## Sources

### schema-gate ###
# Schema Push Detection Gate

> Detects schema-relevant files in the phase scope and injects a mandatory `[BLOCKING]` schema push task into the plan. Prevents false-positive verification where build/types pass because TypeScript types come from config, not the live database.

Check if any files in the phase scope match schema patterns:

```bash
PHASE_SECTION=$(gsd_run query roadmap.get-phase "${PHASE}" --pick section 2>/dev/null)
```

Scan `PHASE_SECTION`, `CONTEXT.md` (if loaded), and `RESEARCH.md` (if exists) for file paths matching these ORM patterns:

| ORM | File Patterns |
|-----|--------------|
| Payload CMS | `src/collections/**/*.ts`, `src/globals/**/*.ts` |
| Prisma | `prisma/schema.prisma`, `prisma/schema/*.prisma` |
| Drizzle | `drizzle/schema.ts`, `src/db/schema.ts`, `drizzle/*.ts` |
| Supabase | `supabase/migrations/*.sql` |
| TypeORM | `src/entities/**/*.ts`, `src/migrations/**/*.ts` |

Also check if any existing PLAN.md files for this phase already reference these file patterns in `files_modified`.

**If schema-relevant files detected:**

Set `SCHEMA_PUSH_REQUIRED=true` and `SCHEMA_ORM={detected_orm}`.

Determine the push command for the detected ORM:

| ORM | Push Command | Non-TTY Workaround |
|-----|-------------|-------------------|
| Payload CMS | `npx payload migrate` | `CI=true PAYLOAD_MIGRATING=true npx payload migrate` |
| Prisma | `npx prisma db push` | `npx prisma db push --accept-data-loss` (if destructive) |
| Drizzle | `npx drizzle-kit push` | `npx drizzle-kit push` |
| Supabase | `supabase db push` | Set `SUPABASE_ACCESS_TOKEN` env var |
| TypeORM | `npx typeorm migration:run` | `npx typeorm migration:run -d src/data-source.ts` |

Inject the following into the planner prompt (step 8) as an additional constraint:

```markdown
<schema_push_requirement>
**[BLOCKING] Schema Push Required**

This phase modifies schema-relevant files ({detected_files}). The planner MUST include
a `[BLOCKING]` task that runs the database schema push command AFTER all schema file
modifications are complete but BEFORE verification.

- ORM detected: {SCHEMA_ORM}
- Push command: {push_command}
- Non-TTY workaround: {env_hint}
- If push requires interactive prompts that cannot be suppressed, flag the task for
  manual intervention with `autonomous: false`

This task is mandatory — the phase CANNOT pass verification without it. Build and
type checks will pass without the push (types come from config, not the live database),
creating a false-positive verification state.
</schema_push_requirement>
```

Display: `Schema files detected ({SCHEMA_ORM}) — [BLOCKING] push task will be injected into plans`

**If no schema-relevant files detected:** Skip silently.


### tdd ###
<tdd_mode_active>
**TDD Mode is ENABLED.** Apply TDD heuristics to all eligible tasks:
- Business logic with defined I/O → type: tdd
- API endpoints with request/response contracts → type: tdd

382	## Open Questions
383	
384	1. **Exact API route shape for D-10/D-11's grant re-mint persistence.**
385	   - What we know: `GrantRemintCallbacks.updateGrantFn(shareId, readDescriptorRef, newGeneration)` needs a backing mutation; `deleteGrantFn` already maps cleanly to the existing `DELETE /shares/:shareId`.
386	   - What's unclear: whether to add a single-grant `PATCH /shares/:shareId` (extend the existing hide/item-name PATCH family) or a purpose-built route; also whether `queryGrantsFn` should get a new `GET /shares/sent?rootNodeId=` filter param rather than client-side filtering of the full sent-shares list.
387	   - Recommendation: default to extending `PATCH /shares/:shareId` with optional `readDescriptorRef`/`rootGeneration` fields (smallest DTO/controller delta, consistent with the existing hide/item-name PATCH pattern) and defer the `rootNodeId` query-param filter unless the planner judges the O(all sent shares) client-side filter unacceptable for this milestone.
388	
389	2. **Whether `packages/sdk`'s `ensureFolderLoaded`/`createFolder` stub-throws (`'not implemented — phase 63'`) block any Phase-68 mutation path.**
390	   - What we know: `moveItem` explicitly does NOT call `ensureFolderLoaded` ("moveItem does not auto-load" per its own comment) and requires both folders pre-loaded via direct `FolderTree.get()`; `renameItem`/`deleteItem`/`deleteToBin` DO call `requireFolder`, which falls back to the throwing `ensureFolderLoaded` only when the folder isn't already in `FolderTree`.
391	   - What's unclear: whether the web app's existing navigation flow always pre-populates `FolderTree` before a rename/delete is possible (likely yes, since the file browser must have loaded the folder to show the delete button) — if so this stub-throw is a latent-but-unreachable path for Phase 68's flows and out of scope; if there is a reachable path (e.g., search-result delete without prior navigation) it could throw unrelated to rotation.
392	   - Recommendation: the planner should NOT scope fixing `ensureFolderLoaded`/`createFolder` into Phase 68 (out of the stated boundary and requirements) but should have the executor verify via a quick manual/E2E check that the phase's own new test coverage doesn't trip this pre-existing stub.
393	
```

**No.** The plan's test scope is unit-level only — it does not touch the web-e2e (Playwright) suite or fix any pre-existing e2e issues.

What the 9 plans actually cover for testing:
- **New vitest unit tests** for this phase's own code: `rotation-state`, `ipns.service`, `share.service`, `client-rotation` (sdk), `notification.store`/`rotation.store`, `multi-tab-lock`, `rotation-driver`, `owner-reconcile`, `useMutationFailureUx`, plus `shares.controller.spec.ts` in `apps/api` (correct — NestJS/jest uses `.spec.ts`; the `.spec.ts` ban is `apps/web`-only).
- **Wave-0 IndexedDB test shim** (`fake-indexeddb`) so the durable-store unit tests can run at all.
- **SC#5 static check**: `find apps/web/src -name "*.spec.ts"` returns empty.

What it explicitly does **not** do:
- No Playwright / `web-e2e` work — zero e2e/end-to-end references in any plan.
- No fixing of existing broken web e2e tests.
- The rotation badge/toast states and the reload-mid-walk resume flow are captured as **manual-only verifications** in VALIDATION.md, not automated e2e.

This matches scope: the phase requirements are ROT-07 + the 5 ROADMAP success criteria, and SC#5 only mandates the unit-test-extension convention (`.test.ts`) — nothing in ROADMAP/CONTEXT asks for e2e coverage or an e2e-repair sweep. Adding it would be an expansion beyond the locked scope.

If you want e2e in this phase, I'd recommend one of:
1. **Add a dedicated e2e plan (68-10)** — Playwright coverage for the rotation flows (revoke → badge lifecycle → reload-resume → fail-closed toast). This is the security-critical path most worth an e2e, but it's net-new scope.
2. **Separate follow-up** — keep 68 as-is (unit + manual), file the e2e work as its own phase/todo. Cleaner given web-e2e only gates on main-push anyway.
3. **Scope it to "fix existing broken web-e2e" only** — different task entirely; needs a triage of what's currently failing first.

Want me to add an e2e plan to Phase 68, or capture it as a follow-up? If you meant a specific set of currently-failing e2e tests, tell me which and I'll scope from there.

Now I understand the full picture. The reality is a bit more nuanced than the doc's landscape table, which matters for the fix:

- `apps/web` **does** have a vitest config + ~10 existing `*.test.ts` files (services/stores/hooks) — but it's **not** in the CI unit-test gate list or the coverage-threshold table. The documented doctrine and gates treat SDK as the home for unit-tested logic and `tests/web-e2e/` (Playwright) as the home for UI behavior — and there's already a rich web-e2e suite (`sharing-workflow`, `writable-shares`, `bin-restore-after-reload`, etc.).
- My plans violated this: they added ~7 new `apps/web/src/**/*.test.ts` unit files + a `fake-indexeddb` Wave-0 shim, and even put the durable-state logic in `apps/web` rather than the SDK.

So Phase 68 needs a test-strategy replan. The one real fork is how aggressively to hoist logic into the SDK. Let me confirm the approach before I spend the tokens rewriting all 9 plans.

  "content": "---\nname: feedback-web-ui-not-unit-tested-logic-in-sdk\ndescription: apps/web UI is not unit-tested; hoist core logic into the SDK (Vitest) and cover UI via web-e2e Playwright only\nmetadata:\n  type: feedback\n---\n\nTesting doctrine for `apps/web`: **the web UI is NOT the place for unit tests.** Core/business logic must live in the SDK (`packages/sdk`, `packages/sdk-core`) where it is unit-tested with Vitest; the web app is a thin adapter/UI layer covered ONLY by Web E2E (Playwright, `tests/web-e2e/tests/*.spec.ts`).\n\n**Why:** `docs/TESTING.md` Test Landscape table deliberately omits `apps/web` from the unit-test suites and coverage-threshold table — SDK is the home for unit-tested logic, `tests/web-e2e/` is the home for UI behavior. When planning a web-integration phase, put logic (state machines, fail-closed enforcement, comparisons) in the SDK behind an injected interface seam; put the platform impl (e.g. IndexedDB adapter), components, stores, hooks in `apps/web` with NO new unit tests; add web-e2e Playwright specs for the user-observable flows.\n\n**How to apply:** When a plan needs a \"durable\" or \"persists across reload\" test, that belongs in web-e2e (a real browser reload) — an in-memory-map unit test gets rejected at review as \"in-memory only.\" The SDK unit test proves the logic; the web-e2e proves durability/UI. Note `apps/web` DOES have ~10 legacy `*.test.ts` files (services/stores/hooks) that exist but are not CI-gated — don't treat their presence as license to add more; don't refactor them either unless asked. `apps/api` IS unit-tested (Jest `.spec.ts`) — that's correct and separate. Caught on Phase 68 planning after I wrongly scattered apps/web unit tests + a fake-indexeddb shim. Relates to [[project-web-vitest-include-test-only]] and [[project-web-sdk-folder-state-desync]].\n"
}

| 4 | 10 | **web-e2e (Playwright)** — `rotation-durability.spec.ts` (real-reload durability + fail-closed toast, SC#1/SC#4) + `rotation-ux.spec.ts` (badge lifecycle + co-writer/defer UX) |

Test strategy now matches doctrine: **logic in the SDK (Vitest), `apps/web` adapters/UI carry no unit tests, UI + durability proven by web-e2e Playwright.** No `fake-indexeddb`, no new `apps/web/src` test files (SC#5 stays satisfied — e2e specs live under `tests/web-e2e/`).
